{"forms":[{"form":"semiconvergents","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The semiconvergents to the continued fraction expansion of a real number include all the rational approximations which are better than any approximation with a smaller denominator.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A kind of fraction.
